# Image prompts: ready to paste

Generated from the Image prompts document by `npm run prompts`. Do not edit by hand.

Make the character reference first and attach it to every prompt (the older one where marked).
Save each image as `src/assets/catalogue/ID.jpg`, for example `H01.jpg`.

## Character reference

```text
Photorealistic character reference sheet of one man, Arjun, shown four times in a row: front view, three-quarter view facing left, left side profile, and back of the head. Arjun is a 28-year-old Nepali man with medium-brown skin with warm undertones, an oval face with a clean, defined jawline, dark brown almond-shaped eyes, thick straight black eyebrows, a straight nose, medium-full lips, small ears that sit close to the head, and a slim athletic build. Calm, confident expression, mouth closed. Natural black hair in a classic taper cut, about 4 cm on top, combed to the side. Light even stubble. Plain black crew-neck T-shirt. Soft, even studio light. Plain, flat warm sand background (#D8CDBF). No text.
```

## Character reference: older version (items marked Older)

```text
The same man, Arjun, at about 50: identical face shape, eyes, nose, ears and skin tone, with light lines at the eyes and slightly fuller cheeks. Natural black hair with salt-and-pepper grey at the temples and through the top. Same four views, clothing, light and background as the main reference sheet. No text.
```

## Character reference: Anisha (women's services)

```text
Photorealistic character reference sheet of one woman, Anisha, shown four times in a row: front view, three-quarter view facing left, left side profile, and back of the head. Anisha is a 26-year-old Nepali woman with fair, light wheatish skin with warm undertones, smooth, even and flawless-looking, an oval face with soft cheekbones, dark brown almond-shaped eyes, softly arched natural black eyebrows, a straight nose, medium-full lips, small ears, and a slim build. Calm, friendly expression, mouth closed. Natural black hair, shoulder-length, straight, worn loose with a centre parting. Clean, fresh, bare skin with no makeup, smooth and even with only a fine natural texture. Plain white crew-neck T-shirt. Professional beauty studio lighting: large softbox key light, soft fill, even and flattering. Plain, flat warm sand background (#D8CDBF). No text.
```

## Hair styles

### H01 Buzz Cut

Save as `H01.jpg`

```text
Photorealistic studio image for a barbershop style guide. Square 1:1, at least 1536 x 1536 px.
ITEM: H01 Buzz Cut
STYLE: one even length all over, machine #2 (about 6 mm); dense, uniform texture with the scalp faintly visible; soft natural hairline at the front; clean, trimmed edges around the ears; neat natural neck line.
CHARACTER (the same man in every image): Arjun, a 28-year-old Nepali man with medium-brown skin with warm undertones, a square face with a strong, wide, angular jaw; forehead, cheekbones and jaw about the same width, dark brown almond-shaped eyes, thick straight black eyebrows, a straight nose, medium-full lips, small ears close to the head, slim athletic build. Calm, confident expression, mouth closed. Plain black crew-neck T-shirt. No glasses, jewellery, tattoos, piercings or hats. Natural black hair unless the item says otherwise. Match the attached character reference for his eyes, eyebrows, nose, lips, ears, skin and build; only the face shape differs, as described here.
TRAITS FOR THIS STYLE (identity unchanged):
- Face: square face with a strong, wide, angular jaw; forehead, cheekbones and jaw about the same width (this style suits a square face, so show it on one; keep every other feature of his identical).
- Skin: medium-brown with warm undertones (his own).
- Hair texture: straight, as in the reference.
HAIR IMAGE RULES: the beard and moustache are the same in every hair image: light even stubble, 1-2 mm, with a clean curved neck line. Main view as the item says. Second view: back of the head, showing the back of the cut and the nape.
MAIN VIEW: three-quarter front, facing left
BACKDROP: Sky blue (#A9C9E8).
LIGHT: soft, diffused key light from the front left, gentle fill from the right, subtle rim light separating the hair from the background. True-to-life skin tones and natural skin texture. Sharp focus on hair and beard detail, 85 mm portrait lens look.
BACKGROUND: one flat, seamless studio backdrop in the solid colour named in BACKDROP, evenly lit like a painted colour-block wall. No gradient, texture, props or background shadows.
LAYOUT: the main view, head and shoulders, on the left, filling about 60% of the width. A second, smaller view in the lower right, slightly overlapping the main view, with a soft fade at its edge.
AVOID: any text, misspelled words, logos, watermarks, brand names, extra people, hands in frame, hats, glasses, jewellery, tattoos, props, gradient, busy or textured backgrounds, harsh shadows, distorted or extra ears, mismatched eyes, a different face from the reference, blurry or smudged hair edges, cartoon or illustration style, 3D render look, plastic skin, oversharpening.
Keep the right side as clean background: the app adds its own labels. No callouts, lines, dots or text.
```
